     1	// Camera rig — Minecraft-style, two modes only:
     2	//   FLY  — WASD/arrows glide on the view plane, Space climbs, Shift sinks,
     3	//          the wheel scales speed. Sink onto the ground and you land walking.
     4	//   WALK — WASD/arrows walk, Shift sprints, Space jumps, double-tap Space
     5	//          lifts off into flight. Gravity applies: switch to walk in mid-air
     6	//          and you fall (on purpose — it's fun).
     7	// Click captures the mouse in both modes; Esc frees it. Before the first
     8	// input the rig idles in a 'cinema' state (the intro orbit, driven by main).
     9	// Feel constants adapted from LAAS (MIT, github.com/Braffolk/fable5-world-demo).
    10	import * as THREE from 'three';
    11	import { heightAt } from './terrain.js';
    12	import { distToRiver, riverLevelNear } from './landuse.js';
    13	import { LAKES } from './geodata.js';
    14	import { pointInPoly, clamp } from './util.js';
    15	
    16	const EYE_HEIGHT = 1.7;
    17	const WALK_SPEED = 4.6;
    18	const SPRINT_MULT = 2.0;
    19	const GRAVITY = 22;
    20	const JUMP_V0 = 7.0;
    21	const STEP_DOWN = 0.55;
    22	const GROUND_ACCEL = 10;
    23	const AIR_ACCEL = 2.5;
    24	const STRIDE_RATE = 1.7;
    25	const BOB_Y_WALK = 0.026;
    26	const BOB_Y_SPRINT_ADD = 0.018;
    27	const BOB_LATERAL = 0.55;
    28	const FLY_CLEAR = 0.9;          // minimum hover height over ground
    29	const LAND_EPS = 0.4;           // sink to within this of the ground -> land
    30	const WADE_DEPTH = 1.05;
    31	const LOCK_COOLDOWN_MS = 1300;
    32	const LOOK_SENS = 0.0021;
    33	const LOOK_SMOOTH = 32;
    34	const DOUBLE_TAP_MS = 320;
    35	const PLAYER_R = 0.38;
    36	
    37	const KEY_ALIAS = {
    38	  ArrowUp: 'KeyW', ArrowDown: 'KeyS', ArrowLeft: 'KeyA', ArrowRight: 'KeyD',
    39	};
    40	const MOVE_KEYS = new Set(['KeyW', 'KeyA', 'KeyS', 'KeyD']);
    41	
    42	function waterLevelAt(x, z) {
    43	  let w = -Infinity;
    44	  if (distToRiver(x, z) < 18) w = Math.max(w, riverLevelNear(x, z));
    45	  for (const lake of LAKES) {
    46	    if (pointInPoly(x, z, lake.poly)) w = Math.max(w, lake.level);
    47	  }
    48	  return w;
    49	}
    50	
    51	export class Rig {
    52	  constructor(camera, dom) {
    53	    this.camera = camera;
    54	    this.dom = dom;
    55	    this.mode = 'cinema';         // 'cinema' | 'fly' | 'walk'
    56	    this.yaw = 0; this.pitch = 0;
    57	    this.yawT = 0; this.pitchT = 0;
    58	    this.flySpeed = 26;
    59	    this.keys = new Set();
    60	    this.vel = new THREE.Vector3();
    61	    this.basePos = new THREE.Vector3();
    62	    this.velY = 0;
    63	    this.grounded = false;
    64	    this.stride = 0;
    65	    this.bobK = 0;
    66	    this.locked = false;
    67	    this.onModeChange = null;
    68	    this.collideFn = null;        // (x, z, feetY) -> [x, z], set by main
    69	    this.jumpAt = -1;
    70	    this.lastSpaceT = -1e9;
    71	
    72	    let unlockAt = -1e9;
    73	    const requestLock = () => {
    74	      const wait = unlockAt + LOCK_COOLDOWN_MS - performance.now();
    75	      if (wait > 0) setTimeout(() => this.mode !== 'cinema' && dom.requestPointerLock(), wait + 30);
    76	      else dom.requestPointerLock();
    77	    };
    78	    dom.addEventListener('click', () => {
    79	      if (this.mode === 'cinema') this.setMode('fly');
    80	      if (!this.locked) requestLock();
    81	    });
    82	    document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => {
    83	      this.locked = document.pointerLockElement === dom;
    84	      if (!this.locked) unlockAt = performance.now();
    85	    });
    86	    document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    87	      if (!this.locked) return;
    88	      this.yawT -= e.movementX * LOOK_SENS;
    89	      this.pitchT = clamp(this.pitchT - e.movementY * LOOK_SENS, -1.45, 1.45);
    90	    });
    91	    addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    92	      if (e.target && (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA')) return;
    93	      const code = KEY_ALIAS[e.code] || e.code;
    94	      if (KEY_ALIAS[e.code]) e.preventDefault();     // arrows must never scroll
    95	      // first input leaves the intro orbit flying
    96	      if (this.mode === 'cinema' && (MOVE_KEYS.has(code) || e.code === 'Space') && !e.repeat) {
    97	        this.setMode('fly');
    98	        requestLock();
    99	      }
   100	      if (e.code === 'KeyV' && !e.repeat) {
   101	        this.setMode(this.mode === 'walk' ? 'fly' : 'walk');
   102	      }
   103	      if (e.code === 'Space') {
   104	        e.preventDefault();
   105	        if (!e.repeat) {
   106	          const now = performance.now();
   107	          const doubleTap = now - this.lastSpaceT < DOUBLE_TAP_MS;
   108	          this.lastSpaceT = now;
   109	          if (this.mode === 'walk') {
   110	            if (doubleTap) {
   111	              this.setMode('fly');
   112	              this.velY = 0;
   113	            } else {
   114	              this.jumpAt = now;
   115	            }
   116	          } else if (this.mode === 'fly' && doubleTap) {
   117	            this.setMode('walk');               // cut the wings: gravity takes over
   118	          }
   119	        }
   120	      }
   121	      this.keys.add(code);
   122	    });
   123	    addEventListener('keyup', (e) => this.keys.delete(KEY_ALIAS[e.code] || e.code));
   124	    addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
   125	      if (this.mode !== 'fly' || !this.locked) return;
   126	      this.flySpeed = clamp(this.flySpeed * (e.deltaY > 0 ? 0.85 : 1.18), 3, 300);
   127	    }, { passive: true });
   128	    addEventListener('blur', () => this.keys.clear());
   129	  }
   130	
   131	  // read the current camera pose into the rig (after intro/preset moves)
   132	  adoptCamera() {
   133	    const e = new THREE.Euler().setFromQuaternion(this.camera.quaternion, 'YXZ');
   134	    this.yaw = this.yawT = e.y;
   135	    this.pitch = this.pitchT = e.x;
   136	    this.basePos.copy(this.camera.position);
   137	    this.vel.set(0, 0, 0);
   138	    this.velY = 0;
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  setMode(mode) {
   142	    if (mode === this.mode) return;
   143	    const from = this.mode;
   144	    this.mode = mode;
   145	    if (from === 'cinema') this.adoptCamera();
   146	    if (mode === 'walk') {
   147	      // do NOT snap to the ground: if you were flying you now fall to it
   148	      const g = heightAt(this.basePos.x, this.basePos.z) + EYE_HEIGHT;
   149	      if (this.basePos.y <= g + LAND_EPS) {
   150	        this.basePos.y = g;
   151	        this.grounded = true;
   152	      } else {
   153	        this.grounded = false;
   154	      }
   155	      this.velY = 0;
   156	    }
   157	    if (this.onModeChange) this.onModeChange(mode);
   158	  }
   159	
   160	  applyCollision(feetY) {
   161	    if (!this.collideFn) return;
   162	    const c = this.collideFn(this.basePos.x, this.basePos.z, feetY);
   163	    this.basePos.x = c[0];
   164	    this.basePos.z = c[1];
   165	  }
   166	
   167	  update(dt) {
   168	    if (this.mode === 'cinema') return;
   169	    const lk = 1 - Math.exp(-LOOK_SMOOTH * dt);
   170	    this.yaw += (this.yawT - this.yaw) * lk;
   171	    this.pitch += (this.pitchT - this.pitch) * lk;
   172	
   173	    const fwd = new THREE.Vector3(-Math.sin(this.yaw), 0, -Math.cos(this.yaw));
   174	    const right = new THREE.Vector3(-fwd.z, 0, fwd.x);
   175	    const wish = new THREE.Vector3();
   176	    if (this.keys.has('KeyW')) wish.add(fwd);
   177	    if (this.keys.has('KeyS')) wish.sub(fwd);
   178	    if (this.keys.has('KeyD')) wish.add(right);
   179	    if (this.keys.has('KeyA')) wish.sub(right);
   180	
   181	    if (this.mode === 'fly') {
   182	      // Minecraft creative: horizontal on the yaw plane, vertical on keys
   183	      let up = 0;
   184	      if (this.keys.has('Space') || this.keys.has('KeyE')) up += 1;
   185	      if (this.keys.has('ShiftLeft') || this.keys.has('ShiftRight') || this.keys.has('KeyQ')) up -= 1;
   186	      if (wish.lengthSq() > 0) wish.normalize();
   187	      wish.y = up * 0.85;
   188	      this.vel.lerp(wish.multiplyScalar(this.flySpeed), 1 - Math.exp(-6 * dt));
   189	      this.basePos.addScaledVector(this.vel, dt);
   190	      this.applyCollision(this.basePos.y - 1.0);
   191	      const eyeGround = heightAt(this.basePos.x, this.basePos.z) + EYE_HEIGHT;
   192	      if (this.basePos.y <= eyeGround + LAND_EPS) {
   193	        if (up < 0) {
   194	          // settled onto the turf while sinking: that's a landing
   195	          this.basePos.y = eyeGround;
   196	          this.setMode('walk');
   197	        } else {
   198	          this.basePos.y = eyeGround; // hover at eye height, don't sink in
   199	        }
   200	      }
   201	      this.camera.position.copy(this.basePos);
   202	      this.camera.quaternion.setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(this.pitch, this.yaw, 0, 'YXZ'));
   203	      return;
   204	    }
   205	
   206	    // ---- walk ----
   207	    if (wish.lengthSq() > 0) wish.normalize();
   208	    const sprint = this.keys.has('ShiftLeft') || this.keys.has('ShiftRight');
   209	    const speed = WALK_SPEED * (sprint ? SPRINT_MULT : 1);
   210	    const accel = this.grounded ? GROUND_ACCEL : AIR_ACCEL;
   211	    this.vel.x += (wish.x * speed - this.vel.x) * (1 - Math.exp(-accel * dt));
   212	    this.vel.z += (wish.z * speed - this.vel.z) * (1 - Math.exp(-accel * dt));
   213	    this.basePos.x += this.vel.x * dt;
   214	    this.basePos.z += this.vel.z * dt;
   215	    this.applyCollision(this.basePos.y - EYE_HEIGHT);
   216	
   217	    const ground = heightAt(this.basePos.x, this.basePos.z);
   218	    const water = waterLevelAt(this.basePos.x, this.basePos.z);
   219	
   220	    if (this.grounded && this.jumpAt > 0 && performance.now() - this.jumpAt < 150) {
   221	      this.velY = JUMP_V0;
   222	      this.grounded = false;
   223	      this.jumpAt = -1;
   224	    }
   225	    this.velY -= GRAVITY * dt;
   226	    this.basePos.y += this.velY * dt;
   227	    const eyeFloor = ground + EYE_HEIGHT;
   228	    if (this.basePos.y <= eyeFloor + (this.grounded ? STEP_DOWN : 0)) {
   229	      this.basePos.y = eyeFloor;
   230	      this.velY = 0;
   231	      this.grounded = true;
   232	    } else if (this.basePos.y > eyeFloor + STEP_DOWN) {
   233	      this.grounded = false;
   234	    }
   235	    // wading: keep the eye above open water
   236	    if (water > -1e8 && this.basePos.y < water + WADE_DEPTH) {
   237	      this.basePos.y = water + WADE_DEPTH;
   238	      this.velY = Math.max(0, this.velY);
   239	      this.grounded = true;
   240	    }
   241	
   242	    // stride-matched bob (amplitude follows actual horizontal speed)
   243	    const hSpeed = Math.hypot(this.vel.x, this.vel.z);
   244	    this.bobK += ((this.grounded ? clamp(hSpeed / WALK_SPEED, 0, 2) : 0) - this.bobK) * (1 - Math.exp(-8 * dt));
   245	    this.stride += hSpeed * dt * STRIDE_RATE;
   246	    const amp = (BOB_Y_WALK + Math.max(0, hSpeed / WALK_SPEED - 1) * BOB_Y_SPRINT_ADD) * this.bobK;
   247	    const bobY = Math.sin(this.stride * 2) * amp;
   248	    const bobX = Math.sin(this.stride) * amp * BOB_LATERAL;
   249	
   250	    this.camera.position.copy(this.basePos);
   251	    this.camera.position.y += bobY;
   252	    this.camera.position.addScaledVector(right, bobX);
   253	    this.camera.quaternion.setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(this.pitch, this.yaw, Math.sin(this.stride) * 0.0032 * this.bobK, 'YXZ'));
   254	  }
   255	}
   256	
   257	export { EYE_HEIGHT, PLAYER_R };

    61	async function boot() {
    62	  const canvas = $('scene');
    63	  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true });
    64	  // 1.75 cap: with 4x MSAA in the composer, full hi-DPI supersampling is
    65	  // wasted fill — this keeps text-sharpness without doubling the pixel bill
    66	  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio, 1.75));
    67	  renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
    68	  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
    69	  renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
    70	  // the sun crawls (4-min day): re-rendering the 7M-tri shadow pass every
    71	  // frame is the single biggest waste — refresh it on a cadence instead
    72	  renderer.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false;
    73	  renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
    74	  renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    75	  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.05;
    76	  renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    77	
    78	  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    79	  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(55, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.5, 12000);
    80	  const steadY = () => heightAt(S.x, S.z);
    81	
    82	  await progress('Sculpting the Vidzeme Upland from real elevation data…');
    83	  initTextures();
    84	  const terrain = buildTerrain();
    85	  scene.add(terrain);
    86	
    87	  await progress('Filling the Gauja and Dabaru lake…');
    88	  const water = buildWater();
    89	  scene.add(water.group);
    90	
    91	  await progress('Raising the sky…');
    92	  const sky = buildSky(scene, renderer);
    93	
    94	  await progress('Growing the forests — branches, twig atlases, impostors…');
    95	  const veg = buildVegetation(scene, renderer);
    96	  await progress('Sowing half a million blades of grass…');
    97	  const grass = buildGrass(scene);
    98	
    99	  // post chain: MSAA render target -> subtle bloom -> tone-mapped output
   100	  const rtSamples = renderer.capabilities.isWebGL2 ? 4 : 0;
   101	  const composerRT = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(innerWidth, innerHeight, {
   102	    type: THREE.HalfFloatType, samples: rtSamples,
   103	  });
   104	  // No bloom: the analytic sky's radiance spans 1-100 and any bloom pass —
   105	  // pre- or post-tonemap — smears it into a white wash facing the sun.
   106	  // MSAA + HalfFloat + OutputPass give clean AA and filmic tone mapping.
   107	  const composer = new EffectComposer(renderer, composerRT);
   108	  // size the composer's targets by pixel ratio — without this the frame is
   109	  // rendered at CSS resolution and upscaled, which reads as grainy on hi-DPI
   110	  composer.setPixelRatio(renderer.getPixelRatio());
   111	  composer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
   112	  composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, camera));
   113	  composer.addPass(new OutputPass());
   114	
   115	  // sky reflections for the water, refreshed occasionally
   116	  const cubeRT = new THREE.WebGLCubeRenderTarget(128);
   117	  const cubeCam = new THREE.CubeCamera(1, 6000, cubeRT);
   118	  cubeCam.position.set(LOC.STEAD.x - 200, heightAt(LOC.STEAD.x - 200, LOC.STEAD.z) + 12, LOC.STEAD.z);
   119	  water.applyEnvMap(cubeRT.texture);

   124	  const mistPts = [];

   126	    const [x, z, y] = RIVER_PTS[i];
   127	    mistPts.push([x, y, z]);
   128	  }
   129	  for (const lake of LAKES) {
   130	    const cx = lake.poly.reduce((s, p) => s + p[0], 0) / lake.poly.length;
   131	    const cz = lake.poly.reduce((s, p) => s + p[1], 0) / lake.poly.length;
   132	    mistPts.push([cx, lake.level, cz], [cx - 90, lake.level, cz + 60]);
   133	  }
   134	  effects.addMistPatches(mistPts);
   135	
   136	  const animals = new AnimalManager();
   137	  scene.add(animals.group);

   140

   147	  const cinema = { angle: Math.atan2(88, 68), r: 110, h: 26 };
   148	  const hintEl = $('hint');
   149	  const applyHints = () => {
   150	    const L = I18N[lang];
   151	    hintEl.textContent = rig.mode === 'cinema' ? L.hintCinema
   152	      : rig.mode === 'fly' ? L.hintFly : L.hintWalk;
   153	  };
   154	  rig.onModeChange = (mode) => {
   155	    document.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.mode === mode));
   156	    applyHints();
   157	  };
   158	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-mode]').forEach((b) => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
   159	    if (rig.mode === 'cinema') rig.setMode('fly');
   160	    rig.setMode(b.dataset.mode);
   161	  }));
   162	
   163	  // the player's body: invisible to the camera, real to the sun. Minecraft
   164	  // says you have a shadow when you stand on the ground — you do now.
   165	  const shadowProxy = (() => {
   166	    const g = new THREE.Group();
   167	    const mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ colorWrite: false, depthWrite: false });
   168	    const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.26, 0.95, 3, 8), mat);
   169	    body.position.y = 1.0;
   170	    const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.16, 8, 6), mat);
   171	    head.position.y = 1.62;
   172	    g.add(body, head);
   173	    g.traverse((o) => { if (o.isMesh) o.castShadow = true; });
   174	    g.visible = false;
   175	    return g;
   176	  })();
   177	  scene.add(shadowProxy);
   178	
   179	  // ------- collision: era buildings/fences as AABBs + tree trunks ---------
   180	  const COLL = { cell: 14, map: new Map(), list: [] };
   181	  const _box = new THREE.Box3();
   182	  function rebuildColliders(group, era) {
   183	    COLL.map.clear();
   184	    COLL.list.length = 0;
   185	    group.updateMatrixWorld(true);
   186	    group.traverse((o) => {
   187	      if (!o.isMesh || o.isInstancedMesh) return;
   188	      _box.setFromObject(o);
   189	      const w = _box.max.x - _box.min.x, d = _box.max.z - _box.min.z, h = _box.max.y - _box.min.y;
   190	      if (!Number.isFinite(w) || w > 36 || d > 36) return;   // bridges, wires
   191	      if (h < 0.5 || (w < 0.26 && d < 0.26)) return;         // props, twigs
   192	      COLL.list.push({ circ: false, x0: _box.min.x, x1: _box.max.x, z0: _box.min.z, z1: _box.max.z, y0: _box.min.y, y1: _box.max.y });
   193	    });
   194	    for (const [x, z, r] of veg.getColliders(era)) {
   195	      COLL.list.push({ circ: true, x, z, r });
   196	    }
   197	    COLL.list.forEach((c, idx) => {
   198	      const x0 = c.circ ? c.x - c.r : c.x0, x1 = c.circ ? c.x + c.r : c.x1;
   199	      const z0 = c.circ ? c.z - c.r : c.z0, z1 = c.circ ? c.z + c.r : c.z1;
   200	      for (let ix = Math.floor(x0 / COLL.cell); ix <= Math.floor(x1 / COLL.cell); ix++) {
   201	        for (let iz = Math.floor(z0 / COLL.cell); iz <= Math.floor(z1 / COLL.cell); iz++) {
   202	          const k = ix + ':' + iz;
   203	          let a = COLL.map.get(k);
   204	          if (!a) COLL.map.set(k, a = []);
   205	          a.push(idx);
   206	        }
   207	      }
   208	    });
   209	  }
   210	  const R_PLAYER = 0.38;
   211	  rig.collideFn = (px, pz, feetY) => {
   212	    const cix = Math.floor(px / COLL.cell), ciz = Math.floor(pz / COLL.cell);
   213	    for (let ix = cix - 1; ix <= cix + 1; ix++) {
   214	      for (let iz = ciz - 1; iz <= ciz + 1; iz++) {
   215	        const arr = COLL.map.get(ix + ':' + iz);
   216	        if (!arr) continue;
   217	        for (const idx of arr) {
   218	          const c = COLL.list[idx];
   219	          if (c.circ) {
   220	            const dx = px - c.x, dz = pz - c.z, rr = c.r + R_PLAYER;
   221	            const d2 = dx * dx + dz * dz;
   222	            if (d2 < rr * rr && d2 > 1e-8) {
   223	              const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
   224	              px = c.x + (dx / d) * rr;
   225	              pz = c.z + (dz / d) * rr;
   226	            }
   227	          } else {
   228	            if (c.y0 > feetY + 1.62 || c.y1 < feetY + 0.32) continue; // duck under / step over
   229	            const ex0 = c.x0 - R_PLAYER, ex1 = c.x1 + R_PLAYER;
   230	            const ez0 = c.z0 - R_PLAYER, ez1 = c.z1 + R_PLAYER;
   231	            if (px > ex0 && px < ex1 && pz > ez0 && pz < ez1) {
   232	              const m = Math.min(px - ex0, ex1 - px, pz - ez0, ez1 - pz);
   233	              if (m === px - ex0) px = ex0;
   234	              else if (m === ex1 - px) px = ex1;
   235	              else if (m === pz - ez0) pz = ez0;
   236	              else pz = ez1;
   237	            }
   238	          }
   239	        }

   468	  function govern(dt) {
   469	    gov.acc += dt; gov.frames++;
   470	    if (gov.acc < 3) return;
   471	    const fps = gov.frames / gov.acc;
   472	    gov.acc = 0; gov.frames = 0;
   473	    if (fps < 34 && gov.level < GOV_STEPS.length - 1) {
   474	      gov.level++;
   475	      applyGov(GOV_STEPS[gov.level]);
   476	    }
   477	  }
   478	  window.__gov = gov;
   479	
   480	  renderer.setAnimationLoop(() => {
   481	    const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05);
   482	    const t = clock.elapsedTime;
   483	    govern(dt);
   484	    if (camTween) {
   485	      camTween.t += dt / 2.2;
   486	      const u = Math.min(1, camTween.t);
   487	      const e = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
   488	      camera.position.lerpVectors(camTween.p0, camTween.p1, e);
   489	      _lookT.lerpVectors(camTween.t0, camTween.t1, e);
   490	      camera.lookAt(_lookT);
   491	      if (u >= 1) {
   492	        camTween = null;
   493	        rig.adoptCamera();
   494	      }
   495	    } else if (rig.mode === 'cinema') {
   496	      // intro: drift slowly around the farmstead until the first input
   497	      cinema.angle += dt * 0.05;
   498	      camera.position.set(
   499	        S.x + Math.cos(cinema.angle) * cinema.r,
   500	        steadY() + cinema.h,
   501	        S.z + Math.sin(cinema.angle) * cinema.r);
   502	      camera.lookAt(S.x, steadY() + 4, S.z);
   503	    } else {
   504	      rig.update(dt);
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    // player shadow: your body is real to the sun while you stand on earth
   508	    if (rig.mode === 'walk') {
   509	      shadowProxy.visible = true;
   510	      shadowProxy.position.set(rig.basePos.x, rig.basePos.y - 1.7, rig.basePos.z);
   511	      shadowProxy.rotation.y = rig.yaw;
   512	    } else {
   513	      shadowProxy.visible = false;
   514	    }
   515	
   516	    const focus = camera.position;
   517	    // shadow cadence: the pass costs as much as the main render — 20Hz is
   518	    // visually identical for a slow-moving sun. The shadow rig only MOVES on
   519	    // refresh frames (sky.update) so matrix and map always agree.
   520	    const shadowNow = ++gov.shadowTick >= gov.shadowEvery;
   521	    if (shadowNow) {
   522	      gov.shadowTick = 0;
   523	      renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
   524	    }
   525	    sky.update(dt, focus, shadowNow);
   526	    veg.tick(sky.state.sunColor, sky.state.ambient);
   527	    water.tick(t);
   528	    WIND.time.value = t;
   529	    grass.update(focus, currentEra, camera.position);
   530	    envAge += dt;
   531	    if (envAge > 5) {
   532	      envAge = 0;
   533	      cubeCam.update(renderer, scene);
   534	    }
   535	    animals.tick(t, dt);
   536	    effects.tick(t, dt, wind, sky.state.sunLow);
   537	    for (const fn of eraTicks) fn(t, dt);
   538	    for (let i = 0; i < birds.children.length; i++) {
   539	      const b = birds.children[i], sd = birdSeeds[i];
   540	      const a = t * sd.s + sd.p;
   541	      b.position.set(S.x + Math.cos(a) * sd.r, steadY() + sd.h + Math.sin(t * 0.2 + sd.p) * 8, S.z + Math.sin(a) * sd.r * 0.8);
   542	      b.rotation.y = -a - Math.PI / 2;
   543	      b.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 3 + sd.p) * 0.12;
   544	    }
   545	    const clockEl = $('day-clock');
   546	    if (clockEl) {
   547	      const h = Math.floor(sky.state.hour), m = Math.floor((sky.state.hour % 1) * 60);
   548	      clockEl.textContent = `${String(h).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   549	    }
   550	    composer.render();
   551	  });
   552	}
   553	
   554	boot().catch((err) => {
   555	  const el = $('loader-status');
   556	  if (el) el.textContent = 'Something broke while building the world: ' + err.message;
   557	  console.error(err);
   558	});

   221	// riķu žogs — slanted-pole fence between paired stakes
   222	export function rikuFence(pts, spacing = 0.55) {
   223	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   224	  const poleGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.035, 0.05, 2.0, 4);
   225	  const total = [];
   226	  for (let s = 0; s < pts.length - 1; s++) {
   227	    const [ax, az] = pts[s], [bx, bz] = pts[s + 1];
   228	    const len = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az);
   229	    const nx = (bx - ax) / len, nz = (bz - az) / len;
   230	    for (let dl = 0; dl < len; dl += spacing) total.push([ax + nx * dl, az + nz * dl, Math.atan2(nx, nz)]);
   231	  }
   232	  const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(poleGeo, MAT.logOld, total.length + Math.ceil(total.length / 5) * 2);
   233	  mesh.frustumCulled = false;
   234	  const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
   235	  let i = 0;
   236	  total.forEach(([x, z, dir], k) => {
   237	    const y = heightAt(x, z);
   238	    dummy.position.set(x, y + 0.75, z);
   239	    dummy.rotation.set(0.6, dir, 0);                            // the slant
   240	    dummy.scale.setScalar(0.95 + rng() * 0.15);
   241	    dummy.updateMatrix();
   242	    mesh.setMatrixAt(i++, dummy.matrix);
   243	    if (k % 5 === 0) {                                          // paired upright stakes
   244	      for (const off of [-0.12, 0.12]) {
   245	        dummy.position.set(x + Math.cos(dir) * off, y + 0.6, z - Math.sin(dir) * off);
   246	        dummy.rotation.set(0, dir, 0);
   247	        dummy.scale.setScalar(0.75);
   248	        dummy.updateMatrix();
   249	        mesh.setMatrixAt(i++, dummy.matrix);
   250	      }
   251	    }
   252	  });
   253	  mesh.count = i;
   254	  mesh.castShadow = true;
   255	  g.add(mesh);
   256	  return g;
   257	}

   612	// palisade ring for the hillfort refuge (Lejstupu pilskalns)
   613	export function palisadeRing(cx, cz, radius = 17) {
   614	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   615	  const geo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.09, 0.11, 2.4, 5);
   616	  const N = Math.floor((2 * Math.PI * radius) / 0.28);
   617	  const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, MAT.logOld, N);
   618	  mesh.frustumCulled = false;
   619	  const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
   620	  let i = 0;
   621	  for (let k = 0; k < N; k++) {
   622	    const a = (k / N) * Math.PI * 2;
   623	    if (a > 5.6 && a < 5.95) continue;                        // gate gap (NE)
   624	    const x = cx + Math.cos(a) * radius, z = cz + Math.sin(a) * radius;
   625	    dummy.position.set(x, heightAt(x, z) + 1.05, z);
   626	    dummy.rotation.set((rng() - 0.5) * 0.06, 0, (rng() - 0.5) * 0.06);
   627	    dummy.scale.setScalar(0.9 + rng() * 0.25);
   628	    dummy.updateMatrix();
   629	    mesh.setMatrixAt(i++, dummy.matrix);
   630	  }
   631	  mesh.count = i;
   632	  mesh.castShadow = true;
   633	  g.add(mesh);
   634	  return g;
   635	}

   879	// glacial erratics — big till boulders dropped by the ice
   880	export function erratics(count, area) {
   881	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   882	  const geo = new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(1, 1);
   883	  const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, MAT.stone, count);
   884	  mesh.frustumCulled = false;
   885	  const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();
   886	  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   887	    const x = area.x + (rng() - 0.5) * area.w;
   888	    const z = area.z + (rng() - 0.5) * area.h;
   889	    const s = 0.4 + Math.pow(rng(), 2.2) * 2.4;
   890	    dummy.position.set(x, heightAt(x, z) + s * 0.25, z);
   891	    dummy.rotation.set(rng() * 3, rng() * 3, rng() * 3);
   892	    dummy.scale.set(s, s * (0.7 + rng() * 0.4), s);
   893	    dummy.updateMatrix();
   894	    mesh.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
   895	  }
   896	  mesh.castShadow = true;
   897	  g.add(mesh);
   898	  return g;
   899	}

   132	// The main roads as real draped ribbons: paint alone lands on 17m-spaced
   133	// terrain vertices, so a 5m carriageway all but vanished. The P30 is a

   135	function roadRibbons(group, era) {
   136	  const mat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({
   137	    color: era === 5 ? 0x393c40 : 0x8d7c5f,
   138	    polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -2, polygonOffsetUnits: -2,
   139	  });
   140	  const positions = [], indices = [];
   141	  const dashPos = [], dashIdx = [];   // painted centreline on today's P30
   142	  for (const r of ROADS_OSM) {
   143	    if (r.c > 1) continue;
   144	    const half = r.c === 0 ? 3.2 : 2.4;
   145	    const pts = [];
   146	    for (let i = 0; i < r.pts.length - 1; i++) {
   147	      const [ax, az] = r.pts[i], [bx, bz] = r.pts[i + 1];
   148	      const L = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az), n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(L / 9));
   149	      for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) pts.push([ax + ((bx - ax) * k) / n, az + ((bz - az) * k) / n]);
   150	    }
   151	    pts.push(r.pts[r.pts.length - 1]);
   152	    if (pts.length < 2) continue;
   153	    const base = positions.length / 3;
   154	    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
   155	      const [x, z] = pts[i];
   156	      const j = Math.min(i + 1, pts.length - 1);
   157	      let dx = pts[j][0] - pts[Math.max(0, i - 1)][0];
   158	      let dz = pts[j][1] - pts[Math.max(0, i - 1)][1];
   159	      const l = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
   160	      dx /= l; dz /= l;
   161	      const y = meshHeightAt(x, z);
   162	      // cambered profile on the RENDERED surface, edges tucked
   163	      positions.push(
   164	        x - dz * half, meshHeightAt(x - dz * half, z + dx * half) - 0.35, z + dx * half,
   165	        x, y + 0.14, z,
   166	        x + dz * half, meshHeightAt(x + dz * half, z - dx * half) - 0.35, z - dx * half);
   167	      if (i > 0) {
   168	        const a2 = base + (i - 1) * 3;
   169	        // wind CCW seen from +y or the whole ribbon back-face culls from above
   170	        indices.push(a2, a2 + 4, a2 + 1, a2, a2 + 3, a2 + 4);
   171	        indices.push(a2 + 1, a2 + 5, a2 + 2, a2 + 1, a2 + 4, a2 + 5);
   172	        // dashed centreline: every other 9m span on the paved P30
   173	        if (era === 5 && r.c === 0 && i % 2 === 0) {
   174	          const b2 = dashPos.length / 3;
   175	          const px = pts[i - 1][0], pz = pts[i - 1][1];
   176	          const py = meshHeightAt(px, pz) + 0.17, cy = y + 0.17;
   177	          dashPos.push(
   178	            px - dz * 0.09, py, pz + dx * 0.09, px + dz * 0.09, py, pz - dx * 0.09,
   179	            x + dz * 0.09, cy, z - dx * 0.09, x - dz * 0.09, cy, z + dx * 0.09);
   180	          dashIdx.push(b2, b2 + 2, b2 + 1, b2, b2 + 3, b2 + 2);
   181	        }
   182	      }
   183	    }
   184	  }
   185	  if (dashPos.length) {
   186	    const dg = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
   187	    dg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(dashPos, 3));
   188	    dg.setIndex(dashIdx);
   189	    dg.computeVertexNormals();
   190	    const dashes = new THREE.Mesh(dg, new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({
   191	      color: 0xc9cdd1, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -3, polygonOffsetUnits: -3,
   192	    }));
   193	    group.add(dashes);
   194	  }
   195	  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
   196	  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
   197	  geo.setIndex(indices);
   198	  geo.computeVertexNormals();
   199	  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
   200	  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
   201	  group.add(mesh);
   202	}

   205	// Background settlement — the real pattern, not one lonely diorama:
   206	//   2025: every OSM building footprint (768 of them, © OSM contributors)
   207	//   1935: the OSM viensēta sites — Latvian farm names persist for centuries,

   211	let bgGeos = null;
   212	function bgAssets() {
   213	  if (bgGeos) return bgGeos;
   214	  const wall = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1);
   215	  wall.translate(0, 0.5, 0);
   216	  // unit gable roof: 1×1 base, ridge along x at y=1
   217	  const A = [-0.5, 0, -0.5], Bc = [0.5, 0, -0.5], Cc = [0.5, 0, 0.5], D = [-0.5, 0, 0.5];
   218	  const R1 = [-0.5, 1, 0], R2 = [0.5, 1, 0];
   219	  const tris = [
   220	    A, R2, Bc, A, R1, R2,      // slope z<0
   221	    Cc, R1, D, Cc, R2, R1,     // slope z>0
   222	    A, D, R1,                  // west gable
   223	    Bc, R2, Cc,                // east gable
   224	  ];
   225	  const roof = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
   226	  roof.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(tris.flat(), 3));
   227	  roof.computeVertexNormals();
   228	  bgGeos = { wall, roof };
   229	  return bgGeos;
   230	}

   644	// spawn helper used by main (handles the special cases)
   645	export function applySpawns(mgr, spawns) {
   646	  for (const [kind, count, home] of spawns) {
   647	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   648	      if (kind === 'aurochsCalf') {
   649	        const rec = mgr.spawn('aurochsCow', home);
   650	        rec.group.scale.setScalar(0.58);
   651	      } else if (kind === 'storkNest') {
   652	        const rec = mgr.spawn('stork', { ...home, r: 0.1 }, { static: true });
   653	        rec.group.position.y = heightAt(home.x, home.z) + 5.75;
   654	      } else {
   655	        mgr.spawn(kind, home);
   656	      }
   657	    }
   658	  }
   659	}

    96	// --- the log building family ------------------------------------------------
    97	export function logCabin({
    98	  w = 6, d = 8, wallH = 2.2, roofH = 2.4, roof = 'thatchGable', old = false,
    99	  hasChimney = false, windows = 0, windowStyle = 'dark', doorEnd = false, porch = false,
   100	} = {}) {
   101	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   102	  const wallMat = old ? MAT.logOld : MAT.log;
   103	  const walls = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, wallH, d), wallMat);
   104	  walls.position.y = wallH / 2;
   105	  g.add(walls, cornerLogs(w, d, wallH));
   106	
   107	  const roofMat = { thatchGable: MAT.thatch, thatchGableOld: MAT.thatchOld, shingleGable: MAT.shingle, tileGable: MAT.tile, barkGable: MAT.bark }[roof] || MAT.thatch;
   108	  if (roof.includes('Hip')) {
   109	    const r = hipRoof(w, d, roofH, roof.includes('thatch') ? (old ? MAT.thatchOld : MAT.thatch) : MAT.shingle);
   110	    r.position.y = wallH;
   111	    g.add(r);
   112	  } else {
   113	    // gable runs along the long axis (d)
   114	    const r = gableRoof(w, d, roofH, roofMat);
   115	    r.position.y = wallH;
   116	    g.add(r);
   117	    if (roof === 'barkGable') {
   118	      // bark sheets are held down by weight poles laid along the slope (Āraiši)
   119	      const slope = Math.atan2(roofH, w / 2 + 0.45);
   120	      for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
   121	        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
   122	          const t = 0.28 + k * 0.26;
   123	          const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.06, d + 0.8, 5), MAT.logOld);
   124	          pole.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
   125	          pole.position.set(s * (w / 2 + 0.45) * t, wallH + roofH * (1 - t) + 0.1, 0);
   126	          pole.rotation.z = 0;
   127	          void slope;
   128	          g.add(pole);
   129	        }
   130	      }
   131	    }
   132	  }
   133	
   134	  // door
   135	  const door = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 1.7, 0.12), MAT.door);
   136	  if (doorEnd) door.position.set(0, 0.88, d / 2 + 0.04);
   137	  else door.position.set(w / 2 + 0.04, 0.88, 0);
   138	  if (!doorEnd) door.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
   139	  g.add(door);
   140	
   141	  // windows on the long sides
   142	  for (let i = 0; i < windows; i++) {
   143	    const zoff = (i - (windows - 1) / 2) * (d / (windows + 0.4));
   144	    for (const side of [1, -1]) {
   145	      const fr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.86, 0.7), windowStyle === 'framed' ? MAT.white : MAT.darkWood);
   146	      fr.position.set(side * (w / 2 + 0.03), wallH * 0.62, zoff);
   147	      const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.04, 0.7, 0.56), windowStyle === 'framed' ? MAT.glass : MAT.door);
   148	      gl.position.set(side * (w / 2 + 0.07), wallH * 0.62, zoff);
   149	      g.add(fr, gl);
   150	    }
   151	  }
   152	  if (hasChimney) {
   153	    const ch = chimney(roofH * 0.8 + 0.7);
   154	    ch.position.set(0, wallH + roofH * 0.45, -d * 0.12);
   155	    g.add(ch);
   156	  }
   157	  if (porch) {
   158	    const p = new THREE.Group();
   159	    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
   160	      const col = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.09, 0.11, 1.9, 6), MAT.lightWood);
   161	      col.position.set(w / 2 + 0.9, 0.95, s * 0.9);
   162	      p.add(col);
   163	    }
   164	    const pr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, 0.08, 2.6), MAT.shingle);
   165	    pr.position.set(w / 2 + 0.75, 1.95, 0);
   166	    pr.rotation.z = 0.28;
   167	    p.add(pr);
   168	    g.add(p);
   169	  }
   170	  return shadowize(g);
   171	}

   173	// klēts raised on posts / stones (Iron Age & later granary)
   174	export function postGranary({ w = 3, d = 3.6, wallH = 1.8 } = {}) {
   175	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   176	  for (const [sx, sz] of [[-1, -1], [1, -1], [1, 1], [-1, 1]]) {
   177	    const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14, 0.18, 0.9, 6), MAT.logOld);
   178	    post.position.set(sx * (w / 2 - 0.3), 0.45, sz * (d / 2 - 0.3));
   179	    g.add(post);
   180	  }
   181	  const body = logCabin({ w, d, wallH, roofH: 1.5, roof: 'thatchGable', doorEnd: true });
   182	  body.position.y = 0.9;
   183	  g.add(body);
   184	  const steps = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 0.5, 0.8), MAT.darkWood);
   185	  steps.position.set(0, 0.25, d / 2 + 0.5);
   186	  g.add(steps);
   187	  return shadowize(g);
   188	}

   196	export function wellSweep() {
   197	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   198	  const fork = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.16, 3.4, 6), MAT.logOld);
   199	  fork.position.y = 1.7;
   200	  const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 0.8, 1.1), MAT.logOld);
   201	  box.position.set(1.6, 0.4, 0);
   202	  const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.09, 5.6, 6), MAT.lightWood);
   203	  pole.position.y = 3.3;
   204	  pole.rotation.z = -0.5;
   205	  const rod = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.025, 0.025, 2.6, 5), MAT.lightWood);
   206	  rod.position.set(2.15, 2.2, 0);
   207	  const bucket = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.18, 0.14, 0.3, 8), MAT.darkWood);
   208	  bucket.position.set(2.15, 0.95, 0);
   209	  g.add(fork, box, pole, rod, bucket);
   210	  g.userData.tick = (t) => {
   211	    const a = -0.5 + Math.sin(t * 0.32) * 0.045;
   212	    pole.rotation.z = a;
   213	    const tipX = Math.sin(-a) * 2.6 * 0.83, tipY = 3.3 + Math.cos(a) * 0; // approximate sway
   214	    rod.position.x = 2.15 + Math.sin(t * 0.32) * 0.1 + tipX * 0;
   215	    bucket.position.x = rod.position.x;
   216	    void tipY;
   217	  };
   218	  return shadowize(g);
   219	}

   671	        const fr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.2, 2.0, 0.12), MAT.plaster);
   672	        fr.position.set(x, 2.1 + fl * 3.1, side * 5.55);
   673	        const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.9, 1.7, 0.08), MAT.glass);
   674	        gl.position.set(x, 2.1 + fl * 3.1, side * 5.63);
   675	        g.add(fr, gl);
   676	      }
   677	    }
   678	  }
   679	  // risalit door + windows
   680	  const door = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2, 3, 0.16), MAT.door);
   681	  door.position.set(0, 1.5, 6.34);
   682	  const arch = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 0.5, 0.2), MAT.plaster);
   683	  arch.position.set(0, 3.2, 6.34);
   684	  g.add(door, arch);
   685	  for (const dx of [-2.2, 2.2]) {
   686	    const fr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 2.0, 0.12), MAT.plaster);
   687	    fr.position.set(dx, 5.6, 6.36);
   688	    const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.85, 1.7, 0.08), MAT.glass);
   689	    gl.position.set(dx, 5.6, 6.42);
   690	    g.add(fr, gl);
   691	  }
   692	  const steps = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(5, 0.6, 2), MAT.stone);
   693	  steps.position.set(0, 0.3, 7.4);
   694	  g.add(steps);
   695	  if (flag) {
   696	    const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.07, 9, 6), MAT.white);
   697	    pole.position.set(11.5, 4.5, 8);
   698	    const flagGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.4, 1.2, 10, 4);
   699	    const fl = new THREE.Mesh(flagGeo, MAT.flagRed);
   700	    fl.position.set(12.75, 8.4, 8);
   701	    g.add(pole, fl);
   702	    const base = flagGeo.attributes.position.array.slice();
   703	    g.userData.tick = (t) => {
   704	      const p = flagGeo.attributes.position;
   705	      for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) {
   706	        const x = base[i * 3];
   707	        p.setZ(i, Math.sin(x * 2.4 + t * 5) * 0.1 * (x + 1.2) * 0.5);
   708	      }
   709	      p.needsUpdate = true;
   710	      flagGeo.computeVertexNormals();
   711	    };
   712	  }
   713	  return shadowize(g);
   714	}

   745	// unlit Jāņi bonfire pyre (lit by the era manager at dusk)
   746	export function pyre() {
   747	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   748	  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
   749	    const a = (i / 7) * Math.PI * 2;
   750	    const log = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.09, 2.6, 5), MAT.lightWood);
   751	    log.position.set(Math.cos(a) * 0.55, 1.1, Math.sin(a) * 0.55);
   752	    log.rotation.set(Math.sin(a) * 0.42, 0, -Math.cos(a) * 0.42);
   753	    g.add(log);
   754	  }
   755	  // the tar-barrel pole beside it
   756	  const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.06, 0.08, 4.4, 5), MAT.logOld);
   757	  pole.position.set(1.6, 2.2, 0);
   758	  const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.32, 0.32, 0.5, 9), MAT.darkWood);
   759	  barrel.position.set(1.6, 4.5, 0);
   760	  g.add(pole, barrel);
   761	  return shadowize(g);
   762	}

   764	// Brežģa krogs — the roadside tavern that bought the manor's ale.
   765	// Latvian krogi were long log buildings under a massive hip roof, one end a
   766	// stable for travellers' horses.
   767	export function krogs() {
   768	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   769	  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(18, 2.6, 8.5), MAT.logOld);
   770	  body.position.y = 1.3;
   771	  g.add(body, cornerLogs(18, 8.5, 2.6));
   772	  const roof = hipRoof(8.5, 18, 3.8, MAT.thatchOld, 0.7);
   773	  roof.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
   774	  roof.position.y = 2.6;
   775	  g.add(roof);
   776	  const ch = chimney(2.2);
   777	  ch.position.set(-3, 4.4, 0);
   778	  g.add(ch);
   779	  // tavern door + wide stable door
   780	  const door = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1, 1.9, 0.14), MAT.door);
   781	  door.position.set(-4, 0.95, 4.3);
   782	  const stable = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6, 2.2, 0.14), MAT.door);
   783	  stable.position.set(6, 1.1, 4.3);
   784	  g.add(door, stable);
   785	  for (const wx of [-7.5, -0.5]) {
   786	    const fr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.8, 0.8, 0.1), MAT.darkWood);
   787	    fr.position.set(wx, 1.6, 4.28);
   788	    g.add(fr);
   789	  }
   790	  // hitching rail
   791	  const rail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.04, 0.04, 5, 5), MAT.lightWood);
   792	  rail.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
   793	  rail.position.set(-4, 1, 6.4);
   794	  g.add(rail);
   795	  return shadowize(g);
   796	}

   798	// the 2017 observation tower on Brežģa kalns (11 m, timber lattice)
   799	export function observationTower() {
   800	  const g = new THREE.Group();
   801	  const H = 11;
   802	  for (const [sx, sz] of [[-1, -1], [1, -1], [1, 1], [-1, 1]]) {
   803	    const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.09, 0.13, H, 6), MAT.lightWood);
   804	    leg.position.set(sx * 1.5, H / 2, sz * 1.5);
   805	    leg.rotation.set(sz * -0.045, 0, sx * 0.045);
   806	    g.add(leg);
   807	  }
   808	  for (let lvl = 1; lvl <= 3; lvl++) {
   809	    const y = lvl * (H / 3.2);
   810	    const s = 3.4 - lvl * 0.35;
   811	    const floor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(s, 0.12, s), MAT.plank);
   812	    floor.position.y = y;
   813	    g.add(floor);
   814	    for (const [sx, sz] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]]) {
   815	      const rail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(sx ? 0.06 : s, 0.06, sz ? 0.06 : s), MAT.lightWood);
   816	      rail.position.set(sx * s / 2, y + 1, sz * s / 2);
   817	      g.add(rail);
   818	    }
   819	  }
   820	  const roof = hipRoof(3.2, 3.2, 1.1, MAT.shingle, 0.3);
   821	  roof.position.y = H + 0.4;
   822	  g.add(roof);
   823	  // stairs suggestion: diagonal stringers between floors
   824	  for (let lvl = 0; lvl < 3; lvl++) {
   825	    const st = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 0.08, 3.6), MAT.plank);
   826	    st.position.set((lvl % 2 ? -0.9 : 0.9), lvl * (H / 3.2) + H / 6.4, 0);
   827	    st.rotation.x = (lvl % 2 ? 1 : -1) * 0.75;
   828	    g.add(st);
   829	  }
   830	  return shadowize(g);
   831	}

   860	export function car() {

   863	  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(4.2, 0.6, 1.8), paint);
   864	  body.position.y = 0.62;
   865	  const cab = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.2, 0.55, 1.65), paint);
   866	  cab.position.set(-0.2, 1.15, 0);
   867	  const glass = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.1, 0.4, 1.7), MAT.glass);
   868	  glass.position.set(-0.2, 1.12, 0);
   869	  g.add(body, cab, glass);
   870	  for (const [dx, dz] of [[-1.4, 0.85], [-1.4, -0.85], [1.4, 0.85], [1.4, -0.85]]) {
   871	    const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.34, 0.34, 0.22, 10), MAT.iron);
   872	    w.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
   873	    w.position.set(dx, 0.34, dz);
   874	    g.add(w);
   875	  }
   876	  return shadowize(g);

   207	function makeInstanced(geo, mat, count, shadows) {
   208	  const m = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat, count);
   209	  m.castShadow = shadows;
   210	  m.receiveShadow = false;
   211	  m.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
   212	  m.count = 0;

   488	  function fillTier(meshArr, heights, arr) {
   489	    // round-robin across variants
   490	    const per = meshArr.map(() => []);
   491	    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) per[i % meshArr.length].push(arr[i]);
   492	    meshArr.forEach((pair, vi) => {
   493	      const list = per[vi];
   494	      const capacity = pair.bark.instanceMatrix.count;
   495	      const n = Math.min(list.length, capacity);
   496	      // over cap: stride-subsample — first-n would drop everything north of
   497	      // some grid row (the lists come from a south-to-north scan)
   498	      const stride = n > 0 ? list.length / n : 1;
   499	      const hRef = heights[vi];
   500	      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   501	        const [x, y, z, h, rot, tint] = list[(i * stride) | 0];
   502	        const s = h / hRef;
   503	        dummy.position.set(x, y - 0.08 * s, z);
   504	        dummy.rotation.set(0, rot, 0);
   505	        dummy.scale.set(s * (0.92 + 0.16 * ((i * 7919) % 13) / 13), s, s * (0.92 + 0.16 * ((i * 104729) % 17) / 17));
   506	        dummy.updateMatrix();
   507	        pair.bark.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
   508	        if (pair.cards) pair.cards.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
   509	        col.setScalar(tint);
   510	        pair.bark.setColorAt(i, col);
   511	        if (pair.cards) pair.cards.setColorAt(i, col);
   512	      }
   513	      pair.bark.count = n;
   514	      pair.bark.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
   515	      if (pair.bark.instanceColor) pair.bark.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
   516	      if (pair.cards) {
   517	        pair.cards.count = n;
   518	        pair.cards.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
   519	        if (pair.cards.instanceColor) pair.cards.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
   520	      }
   521	    });
   522	  }

// Procedural material library — every surface is generated, nothing loaded.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { canvasTexture, makeNoise } from './util.js';

const n = makeNoise(808);

export const TEX = {};
export const MAT = {};

function grainStreaks(ctx, w, h, base, streak, count, horizontal = true) {
  ctx.fillStyle = base;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  ctx.strokeStyle = streak;
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const p = Math.random() * (horizontal ? h : w);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (horizontal) { ctx.moveTo(0, p); ctx.lineTo(w, p + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6); }
    else { ctx.moveTo(p, 0); ctx.lineTo(p + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, h); }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

export function initTextures() {
  // horizontal log wall
  TEX.log = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    const rows = 8, rh = h / rows;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const shade = 96 + ((r * 37) % 3) * 14 + n.noise2(r * 3.1, 0) * 26;
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${shade + 22},${shade - 6},${(shade * 0.55) | 0})`;
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh, w, rh);
      grainStreaks(ctx, w, rh, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)', 'rgba(40,22,8,0.8)', 14, true);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(28,16,6,0.85)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh, w, 2.2);                          // gap between logs
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(230,220,190,0.28)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh + 3, w, 1.4);                      // moss caulking glint
    }
  }, { repeat: [2, 1] });

  // grey weathered log (older buildings)
  TEX.logOld = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    const rows = 8, rh = h / rows;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const shade = 105 + n.noise2(r * 5.7, 3) * 24;
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${shade},${shade - 4},${shade - 14})`;
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh, w, rh);
      grainStreaks(ctx, w, rh, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)', 'rgba(30,28,20,0.9)', 16, true);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,18,12,0.9)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh, w, 2);
    }
  }, { repeat: [2, 1] });

  // thatch (reed/straw)
  TEX.thatch = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#8a713f';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
      const x = Math.random() * w, y = Math.random() * h, l = 12 + Math.random() * 26;
      const g = 90 + Math.random() * 90;
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${g + 40},${g},${(g * 0.45) | 0},0.5)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1 + Math.random();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, y + l); ctx.stroke();
    }
    // layered course shadows
    for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 42) {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(50,36,14,0.35)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, y, w, 5);
    }
  }, { repeat: [3, 2] });

  // old dark thatch
  TEX.thatchOld = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#6a5a38';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
      const x = Math.random() * w, y = Math.random() * h, l = 12 + Math.random() * 24;
      const g = 60 + Math.random() * 80;
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${g + 20},${g},${(g * 0.5) | 0},0.5)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 5, y + l); ctx.stroke();
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {                              // moss patches
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${60 + Math.random() * 30},${90 + Math.random() * 40},40,0.25)`;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 8 + Math.random() * 16, 5 + Math.random() * 8, 0, 0, 7);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  }, { repeat: [3, 2] });

  // birch/spruce bark roof sheets (Āraiši roofing)
  TEX.bark = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#6e5c46';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    // large overlapping bark sheets
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const x = Math.random() * w, y = Math.random() * h;
      const sw = 40 + Math.random() * 60, sh = 26 + Math.random() * 34;
      const g = 88 + Math.random() * 46;
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${g + 18},${g - 2},${(g * 0.68) | 0})`;
      ctx.fillRect(x - sw / 2, y - sh / 2, sw, sh);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(30,22,12,0.5)';
      ctx.strokeRect(x - sw / 2, y - sh / 2, sw, sh);
    }
    grainStreaks(ctx, w, h, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)', 'rgba(230,220,200,0.35)', 18, true); // birch-bark pale streaks
  }, { repeat: [2, 2] });

  // two-tone brick (the 1888 neo-Renaissance new manor)
  TEX.brick = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    const rows = 16, rh = h / rows, bw = 32;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const band = r % 5 === 0; // lighter decorative band courses
      for (let c = -1; c < w / bw + 1; c++) {
        const off = (r % 2) * bw * 0.5;
        const g = band ? 190 + Math.random() * 20 : 118 + Math.random() * 24;
        ctx.fillStyle = band
          ? `rgb(${g},${g - 14},${g - 40})`
          : `rgb(${g + 30},${(g * 0.52) | 0},${(g * 0.36) | 0})`;
        ctx.fillRect(c * bw + off + 1, r * rh + 1, bw - 2, rh - 2);
      }
    }
  }, { repeat: [6, 3] });

  // wood shingles
  TEX.shingle = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#7d6a52';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const rows = 10, rh = h / rows, sw = 20;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const off = (r % 2) * sw * 0.5;
      for (let x = -1; x < w / sw + 1; x++) {
        const g = 100 + Math.random() * 55;
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${g + 14},${g - 2},${(g * 0.72) | 0})`;
        ctx.fillRect(x * sw + off + 1, r * rh, sw - 2, rh - 1.5);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(25,18,10,0.55)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh + rh - 2, w, 2);
    }
  }, { repeat: [3, 2] });

  // red clay tile (manor)
  TEX.tile = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#96412c';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const rows = 9, rh = h / rows;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {

        ctx.fillRect(x * (w / 12) + 1, r * rh, w / 12 - 2, rh - 2);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(60,20,12,0.6)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, r * rh + rh - 2, w, 2);
    }
  }, { repeat: [4, 2] });

  // plaster
  TEX.plaster = canvasTexture(128, 128, (ctx, w, h) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e4d9bc';

      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${180 - g},${170 - g},${140 - g},0.25)`;
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 2, 2);
    }
  });

  // vertical planks (1930s siding)
  TEX.plank = canvasTexture(256, 256, (ctx, w, h) => {
    const cols = 10, cw = w / cols;
    for (let cIdx = 0; cIdx < cols; cIdx++) {
      const g = 122 + Math.random() * 30;
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${g + 24},${g - 4},${(g * 0.6) | 0})`;
      ctx.fillRect(cIdx * cw, 0, cw, h);
      grainStreaks(ctx, cw, h, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)', 'rgba(40,24,10,0.7)', 8, false);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(30,18,8,0.8)';
      ctx.fillRect(cIdx * cw, 0, 2, h);

// Terrain: real Taurene DEM -> carved, painted, walkable ground.
// The height field is shared by every era (the land itself is the constant);
// only the vertex colours (land use) change with time.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { HM_GRID, HM_SPAN, HM_OFF_X, HM_OFF_Z, decodeHeightmap } from './heightmap.js';
import { RIVER_PTS, STREAMS, LAKES, CELL } from './geodata.js';
import { PADS, BUMPS, fieldAt, distToRoad, distToRoadEx, forestDensity, distToRiver, distToStreams, FIELD_COLORS, LOC } from './landuse.js';
import { makeNoise, clamp, lerp, smoothstep, pointInPoly, sampleSpline } from './util.js';
import { SAT_JPEG_B64 } from './sat2025.js';

const noise = makeNoise(1907);
const G = HM_GRID, SPAN = HM_SPAN, OX = HM_OFF_X, OZ = HM_OFF_Z;
const field = decodeHeightmap(); // Float32, row-major, north = row 0

// ---- one-time sculpt of the base field ----------------------------------
function cellToWorld(gx, gy) {
  return [((gx / (G - 1)) - 0.5) * SPAN + OX, ((gy / (G - 1)) - 0.5) * SPAN + OZ];
}
(function sculpt() {
  // channel carving by POINT STAMPING (exact + fast at any map size):
  // each water point lowers the cells inside its radius; candidates are
  // computed from the pristine field so overlapping stamps stay idempotent.
  const field0 = new Float32Array(field);
  const worldToCell = (x, z) => [
    (((x - OX) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1),
    (((z - OZ) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1),
  ];
  function stamp(px, pz, radius, inner, target) {
    const [cx, cy] = worldToCell(px, pz);
    const cr = Math.ceil(radius / CELL) + 1;
    for (let dy = -cr; dy <= cr; dy++) for (let dx = -cr; dx <= cr; dx++) {
      const gx = Math.round(cx) + dx, gy = Math.round(cy) + dy;
      if (gx < 0 || gy < 0 || gx >= G || gy >= G) continue;
      const [x, z] = cellToWorld(gx, gy);
      const d = Math.hypot(x - px, z - pz);
      if (d > radius) continue;
      const i = gy * G + gx;
      const cand = lerp(field0[i], Math.min(field0[i], target), smoothstep(radius, inner, d));
      if (cand < field[i]) field[i] = cand;
    }
  }
  // wider, softer valley profile: gentle grassed banks instead of a trench
  for (const p of RIVER_PTS) stamp(p[0], p[1], 50, 17, p[2] - 1.8);
  // …and a tight channel stamped along the RIBBON SPLINE itself: the water
  // mesh follows the Catmull-Rom curve between the OSM points, which bulges
  // off the point-stamped corridor on bends and left the river beheaded by
  // untouched ground in places
  {
    // stamps every ~5.5m: at the old 11m spacing the r=8 deep zones waisted
    // between samples and the channel bed rose in ridges (the river read as
    // disconnected pools)
    const SAMP = Math.min(3000, RIVER_PTS.length * 8);
    for (let i = 0; i <= SAMP; i++) {
      const [x, z, y] = sampleSpline(RIVER_PTS, i / SAMP);
      stamp(x, z, 26, 9, y - 1.9);
      // shallow SHELF to 16m: guarantees no 17m-cell terrain triangle can
      // bulge up through the bank apron mid-collar (the black-wedge bug)
      stamp(x, z, 30, 16, y - 0.55);
    }
  }
  for (const s of STREAMS) {
    for (const p of s.pts) stamp(p[0], p[1], 24, 6, p[2] - 0.8);
    const SAMP = s.pts.length * 4;
    for (let i = 0; i <= SAMP; i++) {
      const [x, z, y] = sampleSpline(s.pts, i / SAMP);
      stamp(x, z, 10, 3.5, y - 0.75);
    }
  }
  // the mill-pond basin: a real dished bed at the Gauja bend so the pond
  // water body meets the mill and dam instead of hovering on the bank
  {
    const P = LOC.POND, lvl = LOC.POND_LEVEL;
    const RX = 56, RZ = 38;
    for (let gy = 0; gy < G; gy++) for (let gx = 0; gx < G; gx++) {
      const [x, z] = cellToWorld(gx, gy);
      const nx = (x - (P.x + 4)) / RX, nz = (z - P.z) / RZ;
      const rr = Math.hypot(nx, nz);
      if (rr < 1.15) {
        const i = gy * G + gx;
        const bed = lvl - 1.2 + smoothstep(0.7, 1.15, rr) * 2.6;
        field[i] = Math.min(field[i], Math.max(bed, lvl - 1.2));
      }
    }
  }

  // pads (farmyards) flatten
  for (const p of PADS) {
    const cgx = Math.round((((p.x - OX) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1));
    const cgy = Math.round((((p.z - OZ) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1));
    if (p.y === undefined) p.y = field[cgy * G + cgx];
    const cr = Math.ceil((p.r + 24) / CELL) + 1;
    for (let dy = -cr; dy <= cr; dy++) for (let dx = -cr; dx <= cr; dx++) {
      const gx = cgx + dx, gy = cgy + dy;
      if (gx < 0 || gy < 0 || gx >= G || gy >= G) continue;
      const [x, z] = cellToWorld(gx, gy);
      const d = Math.hypot(p.x - x, p.z - z);
      if (d < p.r + 24) {
        const i = gy * G + gx;
        field[i] = lerp(field[i], p.y, smoothstep(p.r + 24, p.r * 0.55, d));
      }
    }
  }
  // lake beds: make sure they dip below their waterlines
  for (const lake of LAKES) {
    let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minZ = Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
    for (const [x, z] of lake.poly) {
      minX = Math.min(minX, x); maxX = Math.max(maxX, x);
      minZ = Math.min(minZ, z); maxZ = Math.max(maxZ, z);
    }
    for (let gy = 0; gy < G; gy++) for (let gx = 0; gx < G; gx++) {
      const [x, z] = cellToWorld(gx, gy);
      if (x < minX - CELL || x > maxX + CELL || z < minZ - CELL || z > maxZ + CELL) continue;
      if (pointInPoly(x, z, lake.poly)) {
        const i = gy * G + gx;
        field[i] = Math.min(field[i], lake.level - 1.3);
      }
    }
  }
  // burial barrows (low mounds — they stay in the land once raised)
  for (const b of BUMPS) {
    const gr = Math.ceil((b.r + 2) / CELL);
    const cgx = Math.round((((b.x - OX) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1));
    const cgy = Math.round((((b.z - OZ) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1));
    for (let dy = -gr; dy <= gr; dy++) for (let dx = -gr; dx <= gr; dx++) {
      const gx = cgx + dx, gy = cgy + dy;
      if (gx < 0 || gy < 0 || gx >= G || gy >= G) continue;
      const [x, z] = cellToWorld(gx, gy);
      const d = Math.hypot(x - b.x, z - b.z);
      if (d < b.r) field[gy * G + gx] += b.h * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.cos((d / b.r) * Math.PI));
    }
  }
})();

// cached lake bounds for cheap margin tests during painting
for (const lake of LAKES) {
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minZ = Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, z] of lake.poly) {
    minX = Math.min(minX, x); maxX = Math.max(maxX, x);
    minZ = Math.min(minZ, z); maxZ = Math.max(maxZ, z);
  }
  lake.cx = (minX + maxX) / 2; lake.cz = (minZ + maxZ) / 2;
  lake.hx = (maxX - minX) / 2 + 40; lake.hz = (maxZ - minZ) / 2 + 40;
}

// ---- height queries -------------------------------------------------------
function baseHeight(x, z) {
  const fx = clamp((((x - OX) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1), 0, G - 1.001);
  const fz = clamp((((z - OZ) / SPAN) + 0.5) * (G - 1), 0, G - 1.001);
  const x0 = Math.floor(fx), z0 = Math.floor(fz);
  const u = fx - x0, v = fz - z0;
  const h = (xx, zz) => field[Math.min(zz, G - 1) * G + Math.min(xx, G - 1)];
  return lerp(lerp(h(x0, z0), h(x0 + 1, z0), u), lerp(h(x0, z0 + 1), h(x0 + 1, z0 + 1), u), v);
}
function microDamp(x, z) {
  let damp = 1;
  for (const p of PADS) damp = Math.min(damp, smoothstep(p.r * 0.6, p.r + 10, Math.hypot(x - p.x, z - p.z)));
  damp = Math.min(damp, smoothstep(14, 26, distToRiver(x, z))); // micro noise must not breach the shore
  // main-road corridors ride a draped ribbon: micro bumps bigger than its
  // crown swallowed the carriageway in stretches
  const ri = distToRoadEx(5, x, z);

To make the instancing finding stronger, I’m doing a non-browser static runtime check with Three.js in Node: build the era groups and count which visible meshes the current collider walker includes versus skips. This does not run the build or modify source.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "node --input-type=module - <<'NODE'
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { buildEra } from './src/eras.js';
const water = { pondLevel: 0 };
for (const era of [2,3,4,5]) {
  const built = buildEra(era, { water });
  built.group.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  let meshes = 0, instanced = 0, included = 0, skippedImportant = [];
  const box = new THREE.Box3();
  built.group.traverse((o) => {
    if ("'!o.isMesh) return;
    meshes++;
    box.setFromObject(o);
    const w = box.max.x - box.min.x, d = box.max.z - box.min.z, h = box.max.y - box.min.y;
    const passes = Number.isFinite(w) && w <= 36 && d <= 36 && h >= 0.5 && !(w < 0.26 && d < 0.26);
    if (o.isInstancedMesh) {
      instanced++;
      if (passes) skippedImportant.push({name:o.name || o.geometry?.type || '"'InstancedMesh', count:o.count, w:+w.toFixed(1), d:+d.toFixed(1), h:+h.toFixed(1)});
    } else if (passes) {
      included++;

    13	  return g;
    14	}
    15	export function placeOnGround(g, x, z, rotY = 0, sink = 0.08) {
    16	  g.position.set(x, heightAt(x, z) - sink, z);
    17	  g.rotation.y = rotY;
    18	  return g;
    19	}
    20	
    21	// --- roof primitives --------------------------------------------------------
    22	// One closed roof solid: eaves rectangle + ridge segment. ridgeHalf = D gives
    23	// a gable (vertical closed ends); ridgeHalf < D gives a hip.
    24	function roofSolid(w, d, h, mat, over, ridgeHalf) {
    25	  const W = w / 2 + over, D = d / 2 + over;
    26	  const R = Math.min(ridgeHalf, D);
    27	  // slight eave drop so the overhang slopes with the roof plane
    28	  const drop = (over / (w / 2)) * h * 0.8;
    29	  const pos = [
    30	    -W, -drop, -D, W, -drop, -D, W, -drop, D, -W, -drop, D,   // eaves corners
    31	    0, h, -R, 0, h, R,                                        // ridge ends
    32	  ];
    33	  // faces: two slopes (quads) + two ends (triangles) — non-indexed for crisp edges
    34	  const quads = [
    35	    [0, 4, 5, 3],   // west slope (x<0)
    36	    [1, 2, 5, 4],   // east slope (x>0)
    37	  ];
    38	  const tris = [
    39	    [0, 1, 4],      // north end
    40	    [2, 3, 5],      // south end
    41	  ];
    42	  const verts = [], uvs = [];
    43	  const push = (i) => {
    44	    verts.push(pos[i * 3], pos[i * 3 + 1], pos[i * 3 + 2]);
    45	    uvs.push((pos[i * 3] + pos[i * 3 + 1]) / 3.2, pos[i * 3 + 2] / 3.2);
    46	  };
    47	  for (const [a, b, c, dd] of quads) { push(a); push(b); push(c); push(a); push(c); push(dd); }
    48	  for (const [a, b, c] of tris) { push(a); push(b); push(c); }
    49	  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    50	  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(verts, 3));
    51	  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
    52	  geo.computeVertexNormals();
    53	  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
    54	  mesh.material.side = THREE.DoubleSide;
    55	  return mesh;
    56	}
    57	function gableRoof(w, d, h, mat, over = 0.45) {
    58	  return roofSolid(w, d, h, mat, over, d / 2 + over);
    59	}
    60	function hipRoof(w, d, h, mat, over = 0.5) {
    61	  return roofSolid(w, d, h, mat, over, Math.max(0.1, (d - w) / 2));
    62	}
    63	function chimney(h = 1.6) {
    64	  const g = new THREE.Group();
    65	  const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.7, h, 0.7), MAT.plaster);

- landing while inside a collider; jump-through-roof; head clearance (collision uses feet-Y gating — can you put your head through geometry?)
- collision AABB build timing: is updateMatrixWorld called before Box3.setFromObject? do instanced meshes in era groups get colliders where it matters?
- player shadow proxy timing vs the shadow-refresh cadence
- camTween vs pointer-locked look fighting
- wading (WADE_DEPTH) vs collision interactions
- KEY_ALIAS arrow handling, LOOK_SMOOTH filtering, adoptCamera edge cases

Successful double-taps also leave lastSpaceT set to the second tap time, so a third Space inside 320ms can toggle straight back; setMode/adoptCamera/blur do not clear that timer.",

A Node buildEra check found era 2 has 22 instanced meshes that pass the same size filter if inspected, including the 360-instance palisade with a 34.3m x 34.3m x 3.0m AABB; main skips all of them before collider creation.",
+      "fix_hint": "Either emit explicit approximate colliders for instanced features such as palisades, fences, and settlement buildings, or iterate instance matrices and build per-instance or merged spatial colliders instead of dropping InstancedMesh nodes."
+    },
+    {
+      "title": "Space used to exit cinema is counted as a double-tap seed, and double-taps are not consumed",
+      "area": "src/rig.js:96",
+      "severity": "med",
+      "evidence": "On a non-repeat Space keydown in cinema, src/rig.js:96-99 switches to fly and requests pointer lock, but the same event then falls through into the Space handler. src/rig.js:107-108 records lastSpaceT for that intro-exit tap, so a second Space within 320ms in fly hits src/rig.js:116-117 and switches immediately to walk. Successful double-taps also leave lastSpaceT set to the second tap time, so a third Space inside 320ms can toggle straight back; setMode/adoptCamera/blur do not clear that timer.",
+      "fix_hint": "When Space exits cinema, return before double-tap handling or clear lastSpaceT. Also consume successful double-taps by resetting lastSpaceT, preferably with separate timers for fly and walk toggles."
+    },
+    {
+      "title": "View presets started while walking return to walk physics instead of fly mode",
+      "area": "src/main.js:340",
+      "severity": "med",
+      "evidence": "The preset comment says camera tweens hand control back in fly mode (src/main.js:335), but flyTo only calls rig.setMode('fly') when the old mode is cinema. While camTween is active, rig.update is skipped (src/main.js:484-505); when the tween ends, src/main.js:491-494 only calls rig.adoptCamera(). If the user starts a preset while in walk mode, the rig remains walk and the next frame applies gravity/walk collision from the preset camera position.",
+      "fix_hint": "Set fly mode unconditionally when starting or finishing flyTo, and clear walk-only state such as jumpAt/grounded as part of the handoff."
+    },
+    {
+      "title": "Player shadow visibility changes can linger until the next shadow refresh tick",
+      "area": "src/main.js:508",
+      "severity": "low",
+      "evidence": "renderer.shadowMap.autoUpdate is disabled at src/main.js:72. The player shadow proxy's visible flag and transform are changed every frame at src/main.js:508-514, but renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate is set only on the cadence at src/main.js:520-523. Leaving or entering walk mode on a non-refresh frame leaves the previous proxy state baked in the shadow map until the next cadence tick; the governor can raise shadowEvery to 5 frames (src/main.js:452-455).",
+      "fix_hint": "Track the proxy's previous visibility/mode and force renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate when it changes, while keeping ordinary movement on the existing cadence if needed for performance."
+    }
+  ]
+}
